TASK: Produce a complete production-ready Python implementation of `tools/aperant_rate_limit_poller.py`. Must include:

1. **Architecture**: 60s cron-style poll loop (asyncio.sleep). Read OAuth tokens from .cli-proxy-api/{claude-*,codex-*}.json. Refresh expired tokens via OAuth refresh endpoint. Poll usage API for each account. Parse response. UPDATE usage_identities SQLite.
2. **OAuth token refresh**:
   - Anthropic: POST https://console.anthropic.com/v1/oauth/token with client_id 9d1c250a-e61b-44d9-88ed-5944d1962f5e, grant_type=refresh_token
   - OpenAI Codex: POST https://auth.openai.com/oauth/token with client_id app_EMoamEEZ73f0CkXaXp7hrann, grant_type=refresh_token
3. **Rate-limit polling**:
   - For each Claude account: GET https://api.anthropic.com/api/oauth/usage
   - For codex Pro: GET https://chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage
4. **Response parse**: extract primary_window {used_percent, limit_window_seconds, reset_at} + secondary_window similar + plan_type + limit_reached
5. **SQLite UPDATE**: UPDATE usage_identities SET primary_window_used_percent = ?, primary_window_reset_at = ?, secondary_window_used_percent = ?, plan_type = ?, limit_reached = ? WHERE name = ? AND provider = ?
6. **Failure handling**:
   - 401 → token refresh + retry once
   - 403 → mark account needs_reauth (could also bump capacity_score=0)
   - 5xx → exponential backoff
   - Timeout → log + continue next iteration
7. **Logging**: append JSONL to Z:/claude-sota-installed/.claude/state/aperant_poller.jsonl with {ts, account, provider, status, primary_used_percent, secondary_used_percent, error}
8. **Smoke probe**: --dry-run flag prints what would be UPDATEd without writing
9. **Service control**: --start (run loop) / --once (single poll) / --stop (graceful shutdown via PID file)
10. **Configuration**: read from Z:/claude-sota-installed/.cli-proxy-api/config.yaml + env-var overrides (APERANT_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS, APERANT_POLLER_DRY_RUN)

132-The app has TWO account management systems that don't fully integrate:
133-
134-**System A: Legacy Claude Profile Manager (Main Process)**
135-- `claude-profile-manager.ts` — Manages OAuth profiles, rate limits, usage, auto-swap
136-- `claude-profiles.json` — Stores profiles with `activeProfileId`, `accountPriorityOrder`
137:- `usage-monitor.ts` — Polls Anthropic's `/api/oauth/usage` endpoint every 30s
138-- `token-refresh.ts` — Refreshes tokens via `console.anthropic.com/v1/oauth/token`
139-- `rate-limit-detector.ts` — Detects rate limits, triggers auto-swap
140-- `profile-scorer.ts` — Scores profiles by availability for auto-swap
141:- **100% Anthropic-specific.** Only knows about Anthropic OAuth tokens, Anthropic endpoints, Anthropic keychain format.
142-
143-**System B: Multi-Provider Accounts (Renderer + Settings)**
144-- `ProviderAccount[]` in `settings-store.ts` — All connected accounts (any provider)
145-- `globalPriorityOrder: string[]` in AppSettings — Manual priority queue
146-- `useActiveProvider()` hook — First account in priority order = active
147-- **Provider-agnostic.** Works for all 10 providers. But has NO usage monitoring, NO auto-swap.
148-
149:**The gap:** System A handles usage monitoring + auto-swap but only for Anthropic. System B handles multi-provider accounts but has no usage awareness.

326-## Appendix: Comparison Table
327-
328:| Aspect | Anthropic (Claude Code) | OpenAI (Codex) |
329-|---|---|---|
330-| **Usage endpoint** | `api.anthropic.com/api/oauth/usage` | `chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage` |
331-| **Auth header** | `Bearer <oauth_token>` | `Bearer <access_token>` + `ChatGPT-Account-Id` |
332-| **Session window** | ~5h | Configurable (`limit_window_seconds`) |
333-| **Weekly window** | 7 days | Configurable (`limit_window_seconds`) |
334-| **Token source** | Keychain (`Claude Code-credentials`) | File (`codex-auth.json`) |

342-## Appendix: Caveats
343-
344-1. **Undocumented API** — `chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage` is internal. The Codex CLI depends on it, so it's unlikely to break silently.
345-2. **Account ID** — May be required. Test without it first. If needed, decode from JWT or read `~/.codex/auth.json`.
346-3. **CORS** — Not an issue (Electron main process = Node.js).
347:4. **Polling rate** — Unknown if OpenAI rate-limits `wham/usage`. Start conservatively (every 30-60s).
348:5. **Multi-account Codex** — Codex CLI doesn't support multiple accounts. We store one token file. If user has multiple Codex accounts, they'd need to re-auth each time (unlike Anthropic which supports multiple config dirs).

import type { ClaudeUsageSnapshot } from '../../shared/types/agent';

// =============================================================================
// Constants
// =============================================================================

const CODEX_USAGE_ENDPOINT = 'https://chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage';

// =============================================================================
// Types
// =============================================================================

export interface CodexRateWindow {
  used_percent: number; // 0-100 integer (e.g., 96 = 96%)
  limit_window_seconds: number;
  reset_at: number; // Unix timestamp in seconds
  reset_after_seconds: number;
}

export interface CodexUsageResponse {
  user_id?: string;
  account_id?: string;
  email?: string;
  plan_type?: string;
  rate_limit?: {
    allowed?: boolean;
    limit_reached?: boolean;
    primary_window?: CodexRateWindow;
    secondary_window?: CodexRateWindow | null;
  };
  credits?: unknown;
}

// =============================================================================
// API Fetch
// =============================================================================

/**
 * Fetch Codex usage from the wham/usage API.
 * Returns raw response or null on failure.
 *
 * Auth errors (401/403) are re-thrown so callers can handle reauthentication.
 */
export async function fetchCodexUsage(
  accessToken: string,
  accountId?: string,
): Promise<CodexUsageResponse | null> {
  // CodeQL: file data in outbound request - validate token is a non-empty string before use in Authorization header
  const safeToken = typeof accessToken === 'string' && accessToken.length > 0 ? accessToken : '';
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    Authorization: `Bearer ${safeToken}`,
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  };
  if (accountId) {
    headers['ChatGPT-Account-Id'] = accountId;
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15000);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(CODEX_USAGE_ENDPOINT, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        const error = new Error(`Codex API Auth Failure: ${response.status}`);
        (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException & { statusCode?: number }).statusCode = response.status;
        throw error;
      }
      console.error('[CodexUsageFetcher] API error:', response.status, response.statusText);
      return null;
    }

    return (await response.json()) as CodexUsageResponse;
  } catch (error) {
    // Re-throw auth errors so callers can handle reauthentication
    const statusCode = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException & { statusCode?: number })?.statusCode;
    if (statusCode === 401 || statusCode === 403) {
      throw error;
    }
    console.error('[CodexUsageFetcher] Fetch failed:', error);
    return null;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// Response Normalization
// =============================================================================

/**
 * Normalize Codex usage response to ClaudeUsageSnapshot.
 * Maps primary_window → session (~5h), secondary_window → weekly.
 */
export function normalizeCodexResponse(
  data: CodexUsageResponse,
  profileId: string,
  profileName: string,
  profileEmail?: string,
): ClaudeUsageSnapshot {
  const primary = data.rate_limit?.primary_window;
  const secondary = data.rate_limit?.secondary_window;

  // used_percent is already 0-100 integer from the API (e.g., 96 = 96%)
  const sessionPercent = primary
    ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(primary.used_percent)))
    : 0;
  const weeklyPercent = secondary
    ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(secondary.used_percent)))
    : 0;

  // Convert Unix timestamp (seconds) to ISO 8601 string for ClaudeUsageSnapshot
  const toISO = (ts: number | undefined): string | undefined => {
    if (!ts) return undefined;
    return new Date(ts * 1000).toISOString();
  };

  // Determine which limit is more constraining
  const limitType: 'session' | 'weekly' | undefined =
    sessionPercent >= 95 ? 'session' : weeklyPercent >= 95 ? 'weekly' : undefined;

  // Use email from the API response if available
  const resolvedEmail = profileEmail ?? data.email;

  return {
    profileId,
    profileName,
    profileEmail: resolvedEmail,
    sessionPercent,
    weeklyPercent,
    sessionResetTimestamp: toISO(primary?.reset_at),
    weeklyResetTimestamp: toISO(secondary?.reset_at),
    fetchedAt: new Date(),
    limitType,
    needsReauthentication: false,
  };
}

// =============================================================================
// JWT Utilities
// =============================================================================

/**
 * Extract account ID from a Codex JWT access token.
 *
 * The JWT payload typically contains a `chatgpt_account_id` or `account_id`
 * field for team accounts. Returns undefined if extraction fails — non-critical
 * because the endpoint works without it for personal accounts.
 */
export function getCodexAccountId(accessToken: string): string | undefined {
  try {
    // JWT is three base64url-encoded parts separated by dots
    const parts = accessToken.split('.');
    if (parts.length !== 3) return undefined;

    // Decode the payload (second part)
    const payload = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(parts[1], 'base64url').toString('utf-8')) as Record<
      string,
      unknown
    >;

    const id = payload.chatgpt_account_id ?? payload.account_id;
    return typeof id === 'string' ? id : undefined;
  } catch {
    // JWT decode failed — non-critical
    return undefined;
  }
}

1469-### Key SOTA findings
1470-
1471-1. **Fill-first selection is SOTA for 8-account 5h-window staggering** (per `selector.go:34-35` upstream comment "burns one account before moving to the next, which can help stagger rolling-window subscription caps"). Round-robin would cause SIMULTANEOUS exhaustion at hour 5 across all 8 accounts; fill-first creates STAGGERED exhaustion at hours 5/10/.../40.
1472-
1473-2. **Session-affinity maximizes prompt-cache hit rate** — extracts `metadata.user_id` matching Claude Code regex `_session_([a-f0-9-]+)$` (`selector.go:430-432`); 1h TTL matches Anthropic 1h cache extension.
1474-
1475-3. **Cache passthrough is automatic + Anthropic-canonical** — proxy auto-injects `cache_control: ephemeral` on LAST tool / LAST system / SECOND-TO-LAST user turn per Anthropic-documented hierarchy. 4-breakpoint cap + 5m/1h TTL ordering enforced.
1476-
1477-4. **OAuth uses official Claude Code ClientID** `9d1c250a-e61b-44d9-88ed-5944d1962f5e` (`anthropic_auth.go:27`) — same client that ships with Claude Code itself. Each `--claude-login` produces a separate auth file; 8 accounts = 8 JSON files in `<auth-dir>/auths/`.
1478-
1479-5. **Codex T1-T7 cross-routing via same proxy** — `OPENAI_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8317/backend-api/codex` (`server.go:367-373`). When Tier 1a codex hooks land, they auto-benefit from multi-account Codex backbone.
1480-

1482-

1485-- 50% prompt-cache hit rate (multi-agent intensive arc): **~24,000 messages / 5h effective** (16× single-account)
1486-- Cost reduction on cached tokens: 90% per Anthropic-documented rate (`claude_executor.go:1851`)

3142-### Critical lessons codified
3143-
3144-1. **RTK `--no-patch` flag is MISLEADING**: it ONLY skips settings.json patching. CLAUDE.md injection + RTK.md creation + @RTK.md reference still fire as side-effects. NEVER use `--no-patch` as dry-run.
3145:2. **RTK `-g` (global) flag writes to operator's actual `%USERPROFILE%`**, NOT redirected runtime — even when CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR / HOME env vars point elsewhere. Sibling-bleed risk EXTREME.
3146-3. **RTK project-local mode FORCES full CLAUDE.md content injection** (~140 LOC). `--hook-only` requires `--global` flag; no minimal-impact project-local path exists.
3147-4. **CR-3 Phase 1 bootstrap exception was VIOLATED** in Phase 5-7 — design-surface side-effects landed before codex T1 fired.

3150-
3151-Pre-install REVERT check + measurement-first dry-run discipline must include reading upstream install-tool flag semantics CAREFULLY (RTK's `--no-patch` documentation was insufficient to predict actual behavior). Future install commands SHOULD test in throwaway worktree FIRST when flag semantics are uncertain.

3153-### RTK adoption decision (post-rollback)
3154-
3155-Per upstream README + dry-run findings, the install paths available are:
3156-- **Path A**: `rtk init -g --auto-patch` — global mode; sibling-bleed (CR-9 violation); auto-rewrite hook on Unix shells only
3157-- **Path B**: `rtk init` (project-local) — forces full ~140 LOC CLAUDE.md injection; no auto-rewrite hook
3158-- **Path C**: NO install — agents invoke `rtk <cmd>` explicitly when wanted (zero install footprint)
3159-
3160-For sss specifically: Path C is the SOTA-discipline choice. Path A violates CR-9. Path B inflates CLAUDE.md beyond /doctor 40K char ceiling (current ~14K + 137 LOC RTK = ~25K, OK now but constrains future expansion).
3161-
3162-**Recommendation**: DEFER RTK install indefinitely. Use rtk commands explicitly when token-savings needed (e.g., `rtk git status` instead of `git status`). Operator can choose Path A / B if/when explicit install desired.
